- **privacy-opsec-suite-fingerprint-resistance**: Use when you need to reduce the fingerprinting and uniqueness surface that could re-link anonymous users. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-fingerprint-resistance.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-full-sweep**: Use when you want the whole privacy-opsec-suite run end-to-end as a guided, checkpointed pipeline. Intra-plugin orchestrator. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-full-sweep.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-leak-incident-response**: Use when an anonymity or privacy leak is suspected and you need to triage, contain, scope the blast radius, and plan remediation without making it worse. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-leak-incident-response.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-metadata-leak-audit**: Use when you need to find personal data or identifiers leaking in logs, telemetry, errors, response headers, or embedded file metadata. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-metadata-leak-audit.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-opsec-hardening**: Use when a LEAK_REGISTER.md exists and you want its leaks fixed safely, each pinned with a regression test. Requires a register as input. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-opsec-hardening.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-opsec-pr-gate**: Use when you want a pre-merge gate that blocks any change adding egress, logging, identifiers, fingerprint surface, correlation, or weakened anonymity defaults. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-opsec-pr-gate.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-privacy-doc-alignment**: Use when privacy promises, the threat model, or opsec runbooks have drifted from code and you want them reconciled into the single source of truth. - **privacy-opsec-suite-privacy-feature-design**: Use when you want high-value privacy and trust features found and specified, each gated against the anonymity model. - **privacy-opsec-suite-supply-chain-trust**: Use when you need to vet dependencies for telemetry, phone-home behavior, and egress, for CVEs, and for build and lockfile integrity under an anonymity-hostile model. - **privacy-opsec-suite-tor-egress-audit**: Use when you need to prove no traffic escapes the proxy or Tor. Owns network egress and routing. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/privacy-opsec-suite-tor-egress-audit.md))
- **privacy-opsec-suite-traffic-analysis-resistance**: Use when you need to reduce observable timing, size, and volume side channels and add padding or batching defaults.
